# Content trace: Japan and the Allied Industrial Base

Each claim of the brief with the dashboard field it comes from, the source datasets and the evidence quality it rests on.

**s1.1** Japan's distinctive role in the Allied Industrial Base is as its specialist supplier and manufacturer: it holds a role in nine of the ten strategic compacts, and 63 economies worldwide count it as their best-placed trusted partner somewhere in the chain.

- `aib.n_compact_roles` = nine | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the trusted network (core members, partners and economies treated as allied), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run
- `aib.n_compacts` = ten | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the trusted network (core members, partners and economies treated as allied), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run
- `aib.recommended_economies` = 63 | posture core cells.p1 (best-placed trusted partner of every economy) | Country strategy choices by technology and supply-chain step (supply-chain need, Strategic Industrial Value, Competitive Position, deployment need, policy alignment), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run
